For AU. I know the term origin isnt used as widely around here anymore but for me it still seems to be a great way for me to systematically approach identifying the correct engulfs, poles etc. If price engulfs then it generally wants to move to the source of the move, kinda thing. So I also saw your "bullish intention ? " as just that. It not only engulfed the origin of that move, in which case I would have just said tg met, but it engulfed the base too, or from a diff. perspective or scale, engulfed this FL you have highlighted in red. So Im hoping it can make it up to the next origin. If so, only question really is how deep will it retrace before then. Also gotta look at both sides, if it takes out the low of this next base then is that a signal for lower? Or could it just be a FO? Or maybe should be looking at what the base is a reaction to, and use that FL instead of the base its self to determine. For AU. I know the term origin isnt used as widely around here anymore but for me it still seems to be a great way for me to systematically approach identifying the correct engulfs, poles etc. If price engulfs then it generally wants to move to the source of the move, kinda thing. So I also saw your "bullish intention ? " as just that. It not only engulfed the origin of that move, in which case I would have just said tg met, but it engulfed the base too, or from a diff. perspective or scale, engulfed this FL you have highlighted in red. So Im hoping it can make it up to the next origin. If so, only question really is how deep will it retrace before then. Also gotta look at both sides, if it takes out the low of this next base then is that a signal for lower? Or could it just be a FO? Or maybe should be looking at what the base is a reaction to, and use that FL instead of the base its self to determine. For AU. I know the term origin isnt used as widely around here anymore but for me it still seems to be a great way for me to systematically approach identifying the correct engulfs, poles etc. If price engulfs then it generally wants to move to the source of the move, kinda thing. So I also saw your "bullish intention ? " as just that. It not only engulfed the origin of that move, in which case I would have just said tg met, but it engulfed the base too, or from a diff. perspective or scale, engulfed this FL you have highlighted in red. So Im hoping it can make it up to the next origin. If so, only question really is how deep will it retrace before then. Also gotta look at both sides, if it takes out the low of this next base then is that a signal for lower? Or could it just be a FO? Or maybe should be looking at what the base is a reaction to, and use that FL instead of the base its self to determine.
